Original Human finding

Human finding ID
human-b05c2cca08eda293
Source
Defect report, row 2; scope: global; element: Region
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
No main landmark defined on the page
Page name
Global
Recommendation for Fix
Use <main> </main> tag to define the main region for the page.
Sr. #
1
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Press 'R' key to navigate to all the regions present on the page. 5. Observe that "Main" region is not present.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.3.1
actual result
The main region is not defined programmatically on the page. As a result, screen reader users might not be able to identify the primary content of the page affectively.
comment
element name
Region
expected result
Screen reader users should be able to identify each landmark region as well as navigate to different regions on the page.

Empty alt attribute provided for Logo image link1.1.1GlobalProgrammatic checksNot caughtCaught

View details

Original Human finding

Human finding ID
human-ef48f659b583d13e
Source
Defect report, row 3; scope: global; element: Image
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
Empty alt attribute provided for Logo image link
Page name
Global
Recommendation for Fix
Provide descriptive alternate text such as "Pristine logo" for the image link using the "alt" attribute. Ensure that the alternate text describes the purpose of the image link.
Sr. #
2
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Navigate to the image link present in the header region. 5. Inspect the code and observe that empty alt text is specified for the image link.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.1.1
actual result
Empty "alt" attribute is provided for "Pristine logo" image link. In the absence of the alternate text, screen reader announces the image link as "Unlabelled". As a result, the purpose of the image link will not be clear for screen reader users.
comment
element name
Image
expected result
Alternate text of the image link should be read out by the screen reader.

Original Human finding

Human finding ID
human-34fa811c363f72c6
Source
Defect report, row 4; scope: global; element: Region
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
Missing accessible name for navigation region
Page name
Global
Recommendation for Fix
Specify unique and descriptive accessible name such as "Primary" using aria-label attribute.
Sr. #
3
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Press 'R' key to navigate to all the regions present on the page. 5. Observe that accessible name is not defined for the navigation region.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.3.1
actual result
Accessible name is missing for the navigation region containing "Solutions", "CLIENT", "ABOUT" and so on links present in the header region. As a result, screen reader users will find it difficult to understand the purpose of the navigation region.
comment
element name
Region
expected result
The navigation region should be identified with unique and appropriate name using screen reader for their users.

Missing unordered list mark-up1.3.1GlobalLLMNot caughtNot caught

View details

Original Human finding

Human finding ID
human-7c0c4f4e692f8672
Source
Defect report, row 5; scope: global; element: List
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
Missing unordered list mark-up
Page name
Global
Recommendation for Fix
Use appropriate list mark-up, such as <ul> and <li> to identify related information. In addition, consider applying styles to maintain the visual presentation of a web page.
Sr. #
4
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Navigate to the mentioned links present in the footer region. 5. Inspect the code and observe that list mark-up is missing for the links.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.3.1
actual result
List mark-up is not used to present related information programatically. For example, Links such as "About", "Knowledge", "Our team" and "Everyday Pricing", "Personalized Offers", "Supplier Collaboration" and so on is not marked as unordered list which visually looks like a list of related items. As a result, screen reader users will find it difficult to navigate to the list and different list items quickly.
comment
element name
List
expected result
Related group of items should be identified as list by screen readers. This will help users to understand how many items are present and quickly navigate to different items of the list.

Issue Title

Page name
Global
Recommendation for Fix

Sr. #
6

Code
WCAG Sc
1.1.1
actual result

expected result

Defect report, row 8; scope: global; element: Link
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
Missing Skip to content link
Page name
Global
Recommendation for Fix
Even though headings are defined on the page and this is sufficient to meet this success criteria, however, in order to assist sighted keyboard-only users, it is recommended that a “Skip to main content” link be provided. This link can be always visible, or it can be positioned off-screen and be displayed when it receives keyboard focus.
Sr. #
7
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Using keyboard navigate on the entire page with tab key. 5. Observe that skip to main content is not available.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
2.4.1
actual result
"Skip to main content" link is missing to bypass the repeated blocks of content. Due to this, navigating the page becomes more time consuming for sighted keyboard-only users.
comment
element name
Link
expected result
User should be provided with a mechanism to bypass the repeated block of content.

Multiple H1 headings defined.1.3.1Page-localProgrammatic checksNot caughtCaught

View details

Original Human finding

Human finding ID
human-bdf7349a02c5faf6
Source
Defect report, row 9; scope: home; element: Heading
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
Multiple H1 headings defined.
Page name
Home
Recommendation for Fix
It is recommended to use only one heading with level 1 on a page.
Sr. #
8
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Navigate to the text such as "Announcing", "Presto", "Client" and so on text present in the main content area. 5. Inspect the code and observe the text are coded with <h1> tags inappropriately.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.3.1
actual result
Text such as "Anouncing", "Presto", "Client", and so on are marked up as <h1> elements inappropriately. The parent-child hierarchy between these headings is not presented. This resulted in screen reader users finding it difficult to understand the page content structure.
comment
element name
Heading
expected result
Only one heading level 1 text should be announced with screen reader for their users
View page decisions: https://pristineai.com/

https://pristineai.com/

Original Human finding

Human finding ID
human-ff0c6235d9d6dd06
Source
Defect report, row 10; scope: home; element: Link
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
Broken link text identified by screen reader .
Page name
Home
Recommendation for Fix
Apply the following changes: • Remove the heading tag from the <a> tag. • Wrap the entire link text as a single link.
Sr. #
9
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Navigate to the mentioned link text. 5. Inspect the code and observe that the link text is coded in separate <a> elements.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
2.4.4
actual result
The link text "Learn More ❯ KAI" are identified as separate heading links. As a result, screen reader users might not understand the purpose of the link effectively.
comment
element name
Link
expected result
The link text should be identified as a single link.
View page decisions: https://pristineai.com/

https://pristineai.com/

Run ID: 20261011T015629-880f3e7c-page-001-2382bb5f

Eligibility classification
LLM
Recorded explanation
Home contains fragmented Learn More/arrow/KAI link and heading markup. Existing link rules check empty names or missing href, not whether nonempty link text is split incorrectly; semantic grouping belongs to the LLM.
Human finding mapping approved by
Fresh audit evidence review (Codex, 2026-10-22)
Approved Programmatic explanation
Home contains fragmented Learn More/arrow/KAI link and heading markup. Existing link rules check empty names or missing href, not whether nonempty link text is split incorrectly; semantic grouping belongs to the LLM.
LLM matching rationale
Together the findings identify the separate Learn More and arrow links and their unclear KAI purpose in the reported section.
VA-DAT Matched Findings
The model recommends clearer names rather than merging anchors; README semantic matching credits the same fragmented link-purpose defect. Empty-anchor checker findings alone do not cover the fragment relationship.

Human finding ID

Source
Defect report, row 18; scope: page; element: Heading
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
Multiple H1 headings defined.
Page name
Presto
Recommendation for Fix
Use heading levels as per specification such as <h1> should be followed by <h2>, <h3> and so on. Even though this is required at WCAG 2.1 at Level AAA, it is recommended that the discussed issue be addressed as screen reader users rely on headings heavily to understand the page structure.
Sr. #
17
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/solutions/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Presto' page. 4. Navigate to the text such as "Presto", "Personalized Offers", "Clearance & Markdown" and so on text present in the main content area. 5. Inspect the code and observe the text are coded with <h1> tags inappropriately.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.3.1
actual result
Text such as "Presto", "Supplier Collaboration", "Personalized Offers" and so on are marked up as <h1> elements inappropriately. The parent-child hierarchy between these headings is not presented. This resulted in screen reader users finding it difficult to understand the page content structure.
comment
element name
Heading
expected result
Only one heading level 1 text should be announced with screen reader for their users

Code
WCAG Sc

actual result

element name

expected result

Original Human finding

Human finding ID
human-e0e5277120a36598
Source
Defect report, row 87; scope: page; element: List
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
Missing unordered list mark-up
Page name
Principal AI Scientist
Recommendation for Fix
Use appropriate list mark-up, such as <ul> and <li> to identify related information. In addition, consider applying styles to maintain the visual presentation of a web page.
Sr. #
86
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/principal-ai-scientist/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Principal AI Scientist' page. 4. Navigate to the texts present under the "Responsibilities:", "Key Experiences:", "Technical Expertise:" and so on sections. 5. Inspect the code and observe that the text are not coded as list.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.3.1
actual result
List mark-up is not used to present related information programatically. For example, text present under the "Responsibilities:", "Key Experiences:", "Technical Expertise:" and so on sections are not marked as unordered list which visually looks like a list of related items. As a result, screen reader users will find it difficult to navigate to the list and different list items quickly.
comment
element name
List
expected result
Related group of items should be identified as list by screen readers. This will help users to understand how many items are present and quickly navigate to different items of the list.
